Bash: Write palette-as-light module and test
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat > sea/pal.py <<'EOF'
"""Palette as light. Each material ramp is computed from a light state; the index canvas never changes.
Sea anchors are MEASURED in the sun state (LESSONS 1.2) and relit physically for other states:
   water colour = body * irradiance * sun tint  +  F_eff * sky   (in linear RGB)."""
import numpy as np, sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from lab import srgb_to_lab, lab_to_srgb

def lab2lin(lab):
    c = lab_to_srgb(np.asarray(lab, float)) / 255
    return np.where(c <= 0.04045, c / 12.92, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4)
def lin2srgb(lin):
    c = np.clip(lin, 0, 1)
    return np.clip(np.where(c <= 0.0031308, 12.92 * c, 1.055 * c ** (1 / 2.4) - 0.055) * 255, 0, 255)

LIGHTS = {
    # E: irradiance on the water relative to the calibrated sunny afternoon; tint: colour of that light;
    # sky: sky colour reflected at the 30 deg view; Feff: effective reflectance (Fresnel ~6% + roughness);
    # sun_el/az: for foam lit sides and glitter; haze: veil (colour, amount) for fog/smoke
    'sun':      dict(E=1.00, tint=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),   sky=(72, -4, -24), Feff=0.06, sun_el=50, sun_az=205, direct=0.75, haze=None),
    'overcast': dict(E=0.40, tint=(0.97, 1.0, 1.03), sky=(80, -1, -3),  Feff=0.09, sun_el=50, sun_az=205, direct=0.0, haze=None),
    'golden':   dict(E=0.42, tint=(1.10, 0.86, 0.62), sky=(74, 2, -8),  Feff=0.07, sun_el=9, sun_az=285, direct=0.8, haze=None),
    'dusk':     dict(E=0.10, tint=(1.0, 0.62, 0.62), sky=(52, 12, -22), Feff=0.09, sun_el=-2, sun_az=295, direct=0.0, haze=None),
    'fog':      dict(E=0.45, tint=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),   sky=(84, -1, -2),  Feff=0.10, sun_el=40, sun_az=205, direct=0.0, haze=((82, -1, -2), 0.45)),
}

# measured sun-state anchors (L*, a*, b*)
SEA_DEEP = (34, -9, -17)
SEA_SAND_SHALLOW = (64, -15, -5)
SEA_ROCK_SHALLOW = (52, -4, -16)
D0 = 6.0           # m: e-folding depth of the bottom's contribution (fit: sand 4 m -> L* 49, 17 m -> 36)
KELP = [(24, 4, -14), (30, 2, -18), (37, -6, -17)]          # canopy core / mid / edge-speck (measured 23-45)
AER = [(56, -12, -10), (64, -10, -10), (72, -8, -8)]          # bubble plume (measured 64, sd 18)
FOAM_ALBEDO = 0.82

DEPTH_EDGES = np.array([0.7, 1.6, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 13.0])      # step 6 (shallowest) .. 0 (deep)
STEP_DEPTH = np.array([18.0, 10.0, 6.5, 4.0, 2.2, 1.1, 0.4])   # representative depth of each step

def sea_body_lab(d, rock):
    w = 1 - np.exp(-np.asarray(d, float) / D0)
    sh = np.array(SEA_SAND_SHALLOW) * (1 - rock) + np.array(SEA_ROCK_SHALLOW) * rock
    return sh * (1 - w)[..., None] + np.array(SEA_DEEP) * w[..., None] if np.ndim(d) else sh * (1 - w) + np.array(SEA_DEEP) * w

def relight(lab_sun, L):
    """sun-state measured colour -> this light state."""
    ref = LIGHTS['sun']
    lin = lab2lin(lab_sun)
    sky_s = lab2lin(ref['sky']) * ref['Feff']
    body = np.maximum(lin - sky_s, 0)                 # remove the sunny sky reflection
    out = body * L['E'] * np.array(L['tint']) + lab2lin(L['sky']) * L['Feff']
    if L['haze']:
        hc, ha = L['haze']; out = out * (1 - ha) + lab2lin(hc) * ha
    return out

def foam_colours(L):
    """foam: 0 shadow (sky only), 1 turned away, 2 lit, 3 highlight."""
    sky = lab2lin(L['sky']); sunc = np.array(L['tint']) * L['E']
    amb = sky * 0.55 * max(L['E'], 0.25) / 0.55 * 0.9 + 0.08 * sunc
    dir_ = sunc * L['direct']
    cols = [FOAM_ALBEDO * (amb * 0.75), FOAM_ALBEDO * (amb + 0.35 * dir_), FOAM_ALBEDO * (amb + 0.8 * dir_),
            FOAM_ALBEDO * (amb + 1.05 * dir_) + 0.03]
    if L['direct'] == 0:     # flat light: foam is the brightest thing, but only slightly above grey
        base = FOAM_ALBEDO * (lab2lin(L['sky']) * 0.95) * max(L['E'] / 0.4, 0.3)
        cols = [base * 0.62, base * 0.78, base * 0.92, base * 1.0]
    if L['haze']:
        hc, ha = L['haze']; cols = [c * (1 - ha * 0.6) + lab2lin(hc) * ha * 0.6 for c in cols]
    return [np.clip(c, 0, 1) for c in cols]

# ---- land (context only; rock/forest are other studies' subjects) ----
LAND = {
    'rock':   [(24, 2, 4), (33, 2, 6), (44, 3, 9), (56, 3, 11), (66, 2, 10)],
    'wetrock':[(18, 0, 0), (26, 0, 2), (34, 0, 3)],
    'sand':   [(62, 1, 8), (72, 1, 10), (80, 1, 11), (87, 1, 10)],
    'wetsand':[(46, 0, 3), (54, 0, 4), (62, -1, 2)],
    'gravel': [(40, 0, 3), (52, 0, 4), (62, 0, 5), (70, 0, 4)],
    'forest': [(10, -6, 4), (17, -12, 8), (26, -16, 14), (36, -18, 20)],
}

def build(light='sun'):
    """returns dict ramp -> array of sRGB colours (steps)."""
    L = LIGHTS[light]
    P = {}
    P['sea_sand'] = np.array([lin2srgb(relight(sea_body_lab(d, 0.0), L)) for d in STEP_DEPTH])
    P['sea_rock'] = np.array([lin2srgb(relight(sea_body_lab(d, 1.0), L)) for d in STEP_DEPTH])
    P['kelp'] = np.array([lin2srgb(relight(c, L)) for c in KELP])
    P['aer'] = np.array([lin2srgb(relight(c, L)) for c in AER])
    P['foam'] = np.array([lin2srgb(c) for c in foam_colours(L)])
    sunE = L['E']; tint = np.array(L['tint'])
    for k, cols in LAND.items():
        out = []
        for c in cols:
            lin = lab2lin(c) * (0.35 + 0.65 * sunE) * (tint * 0.5 + 0.5)
            if L['haze']:
                hc, ha = L['haze']; lin = lin * (1 - ha) + lab2lin(hc) * ha
            out.append(lin2srgb(lin))
        P[k] = np.array(out)
    # glint: the sun mirrored
    P['glint'] = np.array([lin2srgb(np.clip(np.array(L['tint']) * 0.9 + 0.1, 0, 1)), lin2srgb(np.clip(lab2lin(L['sky']) * 1.1, 0, 1))])
    return P

RAMPS = ['sea_sand', 'sea_rock', 'kelp', 'aer', 'foam', 'rock', 'wetrock', 'sand', 'wetsand', 'gravel', 'forest', 'glint']
RID = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(RAMPS)}

def to_rgb(ramp_idx, step_idx, light='sun'):
    P = build(light)
    out = np.zeros(ramp_idx.shape + (3,), np.uint8)
    for k, i in RID.items():
        m = ramp_idx == i
        if m.any():
            cols = P[k]; s = np.clip(step_idx[m], 0, len(cols) - 1)
            out[m] = cols[s].astype(np.uint8)
    return out
EOF

Bash: Write the sea painter
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat > sea/paint.py <<'EOF'
"""The sea painter: G-buffer + sea state -> (ramp, step) index canvas. Every mark has a trigger in a field.
Scale-aware: world-space features, and each decides by its size in pixels how it is drawn."""
import numpy as np
from scipy import ndimage as nd
from .waves import bilinear
from .noise import hash2
from .pal import RID, DEPTH_EDGES

BAYER4 = np.array([[0, 8, 2, 10], [12, 4, 14, 6], [3, 11, 1, 9], [15, 7, 13, 5]]) / 16.0 + 1 / 32

def bayer(H, W):
    return np.tile(BAYER4, (H // 4 + 1, W // 4 + 1))[:H, :W]

def vnoise(x, y, cell, seed):
    """value noise in world coords (smooth, 0..1)."""
    gx, gy = x / cell, y / cell
    x0, y0 = np.floor(gx), np.floor(gy); fx, fy = gx - x0, gy - y0
    fx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx); fy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy)
    a = hash2(x0, y0, seed); b = hash2(x0 + 1, y0, seed); c = hash2(x0, y0 + 1, seed); d = hash2(x0 + 1, y0 + 1, seed)
    return (a * (1 - fx) + b * fx) * (1 - fy) + (c * (1 - fx) + d * fx) * fy

def fbmw(x, y, cell, seed, oct=3):
    s = 0; a = 1; t = 0
    for o in range(oct):
        s = s + a * vnoise(x, y, cell / 2 ** o, seed + o * 17); t += a; a *= 0.5
    return s / t

def worley(x, y, cell, seed, ux=None, uy=None, stretch=1.0):
    """F1, F2 (in cell units). Optional anisotropy: cells stretched `stretch`x along (ux, uy)."""
    if ux is not None and stretch != 1.0:
        a = x * ux + y * uy; b = -x * uy + y * ux
        x, y = a / stretch, b
    gx, gy = x / cell, y / cell
    ix, iy = np.floor(gx), np.floor(gy)
    f1 = np.full(gx.shape, 9.0); f2 = np.full(gx.shape, 9.0)
    for dy in (-1, 0, 1):
        for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
            cx, cy = ix + dx, iy + dy
            px = cx + hash2(cx, cy, seed); py = cy + hash2(cx, cy, seed + 1)
            dd = np.hypot(gx - px, gy - py)
            f2 = np.where(dd < f1, f1, np.minimum(f2, dd)); f1 = np.minimum(f1, dd)
    return f1, f2

def sample(F, G, key):
    return bilinear(F[key], G['x'], G['y'])

def paint_sea(G, S, cam, P=None):
    """S: dict of fields on the 1 m grid (depth, rock, kelp, W, F, A, q, Hinst, brk, u, v, T, H, kx, ky, tt).
    P: parameters. Returns ramp, step (int arrays), and a dict of layers for inspection."""
    P = dict(dict(band_jitter=0.35, lace_cell=2.2, lace_stretch=3.0, lump_cell=0.9, kelp_on=0.40,
                  speck_on=0.14, aer_on=0.22, roller_q=0.035, face_q=0.16, chop=0.5, sun_az=205, sun_el=50,
                  glint=0.0, wind_dir=(0.0, 1.0)), **(P or {}))
    H_, W_ = G['x'].shape
    mpp = cam.mpp
    x, y = G['x'], G['y']
    sea = G['sea'].copy()
    ramp = np.full((H_, W_), -1, int); step = np.zeros((H_, W_), int)
    by = bayer(H_, W_)
    d = sample(S, G, 'depth'); rock = sample(S, G, 'rock'); kelp = sample(S, G, 'kelp')
    Wf = sample(S, G, 'W'); Ff = sample(S, G, 'F'); Af = sample(S, G, 'A')
    Hs = sample(S, G, 'Hinst'); u = sample(S, G, 'u'); v = sample(S, G, 'v')
    T = S['T']
    # phase: recompute from travel time so it is smooth at any zoom
    q = np.mod((S['t_now'] - sample(S, G, 'tt')) / T, 1.0)
    brk = sample(S, G, 'brkf') > 0.5
    sea &= d > 0.02
    # ---------------- 1. the body: depth bands over bottom type -------------------------------
    jit = (fbmw(x, y, 9.0, 5) - 0.5) * 2 * P['band_jitter']            # irregular band edges, world space
    lnd = np.log(np.maximum(d, 0.05))
    edges = np.log(DEPTH_EDGES)
    cont = np.interp(lnd + jit * 0.35, edges, np.arange(len(edges)) + 0.5, left=0, right=len(edges))
    # cont: 0 (shallow) .. 6 (deep) continuous; step 6 = shallowest
    frac = cont - np.floor(cont)
    base = np.floor(cont) + (frac > (0.5 + 0.9 * (by - 0.5)) * 1.0) * 0      # placeholder
    # seam checker only within +-0.12 of a boundary, flat elsewhere
    near = np.abs(frac - 0.5) > 0.38
    ck = ((np.arange(W_)[None, :] + np.arange(H_)[:, None]) % 2).astype(bool)
    lvl = np.round(cont - 0.5).astype(int)
    lvl = np.where(near & ck & (frac > 0.5), lvl + 0, lvl)
    lvl = np.clip(lvl, 0, 6)
    body_step = 6 - lvl
    is_rock = rock > 0.5 + 0.25 * (by - 0.5)
    ramp[sea] = np.where(is_rock, RID['sea_rock'], RID['sea_sand'])[sea]
    step[sea] = body_step[sea]
    # rock bottom seen through shallow water: mottle (pale rock / dark weed), fading with depth
    vis = np.exp(-d / 3.0) * (rock > 0.4)
    mott = fbmw(x, y, 2.5, 21, 3)
    step = np.where(sea & (vis > 0.15) & (mott > 0.62), np.minimum(step + 1, 6), step)
    step = np.where(sea & (vis > 0.15) & (mott < 0.30), np.maximum(step - 1, 0), step)
    # ---------------- 2. the swell: broad value undulation + the face before a crest -----------
    # the face (shoreward of the crest, about to be reached) turns darker where the wave is steep
    steep = np.clip(Hs / np.maximum(d, 0.3), 0, 1.2)
    face = sea & (q > 1 - P['face_q']) & (steep > 0.35) & ~brk
    step = np.where(face & (by < np.clip((steep - 0.35) * 2.5, 0, 1)), np.maximum(step - 1, 0), step)
    # back of the wave (just passed): slightly lighter (tilted to the sky)
    back = sea & (q < 0.18) & (q > P['roller_q']) & (steep > 0.3)
    step = np.where(back & (by < 0.35), np.minimum(step + 1, 6), step)
    # ---------------- 3. chop: short strokes along the crests of wind ripples -----------------
    # world-space ripple cells ~ 0.6-3 m; a mark only where its length is >= 2 px
    ux, uy = P['wind_dir']
    cellc = max(1.2, 2.5 * mpp)
    f1, f2 = worley(x, y, cellc, 91, ux, uy, 0.35)
    chop_m = sea & (f2 - f1 < 0.10) & (vnoise(x, y, 25, 3) < P['chop']) & (d > 1.0)
    step = np.where(chop_m & (hash2(np.floor(x / cellc), np.floor(y / cellc), 7) > 0.5), np.minimum(step + 1, 6), step)
    # ---------------- 4. kelp canopy -----------------------------------------------------------
    kn = kelp + (fbmw(x, y, 1.6, 33, 2) - 0.5) * 0.35
    km = sea & (kn > P['kelp_on'])
    ramp[km] = RID['kelp']
    kst = np.where(kn > P['kelp_on'] + 0.25, 0, np.where(kn > P['kelp_on'] + 0.08, 1, 2))
    step[km] = kst[km]
    # scattered floats and blades beyond the mat: specks, a few px, streaming down-current
    speck_cell = max(1.5, 1.5 * mpp)
    sf1, _ = worley(x, y, speck_cell, 44, u / (np.hypot(u, v) + 1e-6), v / (np.hypot(u, v) + 1e-6), 2.0)
    sp = sea & ~km & (kelp > P['speck_on']) & (sf1 < 0.18 + 0.25 * kelp)
    ramp[sp] = RID['kelp']; step[sp] = 1
    # ---------------- 5. aeration: jade halo of bubbles ---------------------------------------
    an = Af * (0.75 + 0.5 * fbmw(x, y, 3.0, 55, 2))
    am = sea & (an > P['aer_on'])
    ramp[am] = RID['aer']
    step[am] = np.clip(((an - P['aer_on']) / 0.35 + (by - 0.5) * 0.8).astype(int), 0, 2)[am]
    # ---------------- 6. subsided foam: lace with growing oval holes, drawn out by the current --
    un = np.hypot(u, v) + 1e-6
    lc = max(P['lace_cell'], 2.2 * mpp)
    lf1, lf2 = worley(x, y, lc, 66, u / un, v / un, P['lace_stretch'])
    # a hole's radius grows as the foam ages (F falls): foam survives where the cell edge is near
    edge = lf2 - lf1                           # 0 on cell borders
    Fe = Ff * (0.8 + 0.4 * fbmw(x, y, 6.0, 67, 2))
    lace = sea & (edge < np.clip(Fe * 0.9, 0, 1.0) ** 0.8 * 0.9) & (Fe > 0.05)
    dense = sea & (Fe > 0.9)
    lace |= dense & (edge < 0.9)
    ramp[lace] = RID['foam']; step[lace] = np.where(Fe > 0.6, 2, 1)[lace]
    # ---------------- 7. whitewater: thick creamy foam in lumps with lit and shaded sides ------
    lump = fbmw(x, y, max(P['lump_cell'], 1.2 * mpp), 77, 3)
    Wm = sea & (Wf * (0.6 + 0.8 * lump) > 0.32)
    ramp[Wm] = RID['foam']
    # lit side of lumps: gradient of lump noise toward the sun
    saz = np.radians(P['sun_az'])
    sx_, sy_ = np.sin(saz), -np.cos(saz)                    # toward the sun, grid coords (y south)
    e = 0.35 * max(P['lump_cell'], 1.2 * mpp)
    lg = fbmw(x + sx_ * e, y + sy_ * e, max(P['lump_cell'], 1.2 * mpp), 77, 3) - lump
    wst = np.where(lg > 0.02, 3, np.where(lg < -0.03, 1, 2))
    wst = np.where(Wf < 0.45, np.minimum(wst, 2), wst)
    step[Wm] = wst[Wm]
    # ---------------- 8. the roller: the breaking crest itself, a thin bright line -------------
    rq = np.maximum(P['roller_q'], 0.6 * mpp / (np.maximum(np.sqrt(9.81 * np.maximum(d, 0.2)), 1) * T))
    roller = sea & brk & (q < rq)
    ramp[roller] = RID['foam']; step[roller] = 3
    # shadow under the lip: the pixel just shoreward of the roller (facing away from the sun)
    return ramp, step, dict(q=q, d=d, Wf=Wf, Ff=Ff, Af=Af, kelp=kelp, brk=brk, sea=sea)

def paint_land(G, S, cam, ramp, step, tide=0.45, sun=(205, 50)):
    L = ~G['sea']
    x, y, z = G['x'], G['y'], G['z']
    rough = sample(S, G, 'rough'); chm = sample(S, G, 'chm')
    saz, sel = np.radians(sun[0]), np.radians(sun[1])
    sv = np.array([np.sin(saz) * np.cos(sel), -np.cos(saz) * np.cos(sel), np.sin(sel)])
    ndl = G['nx'] * sv[0] + G['ny'] * sv[1] + G['nz'] * sv[2]
    slope = 1 - G['nz']
    by = bayer(*x.shape)
    zb = z - chm                                   # bare earth under the canopy
    forest = L & (chm > 3)
    wet = L & ~forest & (zb < tide + 1.6)
    rockm = L & ~forest & ((rough > 0.12) | (slope > 0.25))
    sandm = L & ~forest & ~rockm & (zb < 9)
    grav = L & ~forest & ~rockm & ~sandm
    sh = np.clip(ndl * 1.1 + (by - 0.5) * 0.25, 0, 1)
    def put(m, name, n):
        ramp[m] = RID[name]; step[m] = np.clip((sh[m] * n).astype(int), 0, n - 1)
    put(forest, 'forest', 4)
    # forest: crowns as lit caps at their own scale
    cap = fbmw(x, y, 3.5, 101, 2)
    step[forest] = np.clip(step[forest] + (cap[forest] > 0.62) - (cap[forest] < 0.35), 0, 3)
    put(rockm & ~wet, 'rock', 5); put(rockm & wet, 'wetrock', 3)
    put(sandm & ~wet, 'sand', 4); put(sandm & wet, 'wetsand', 3); put(grav, 'gravel', 4)
    return ramp, step
'''

cat > stage1.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage 1: the sea body, kelp and surf over the real Point of Arches, iso 1 m/px, beside the photo
warped into the very same view through the sea-plane homography."""
import sys, time; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from sea.place import load
from sea.render import Cam, gbuffer
from sea.paint import paint_sea, paint_land
from sea.pal import to_rgb
from sea.sim import bottom_type
from dem import window
from reg import apply
def fields(tag='poa'):
    P=load('poa'); Wv=dict(np.load('../cache/poa_waves.npz')); St=dict(np.load('../cache/poa_state.npz'))
    chm=window('olycoast','chm',372347+350,5346016-1650,372347+1650,5346016-250,dtype=np.uint8)
    rock,rough=bottom_type(P['z'],P['depth'])
    S=dict(depth=np.nan_to_num(P['depth'],nan=30.0),rock=rock,rough=rough,kelp=St['kelp'],W=St['W'],F=St['F'],A=St['A'],
           Hinst=St['Hinst'],u=St['u'],v=St['v'],tt=Wv['t'],brkf=St['brk'].astype(np.float32),T=8.3,t_now=36*8.3,chm=np.nan_to_num(chm))
    zland=np.nan_to_num(P['z'],nan=-40)+np.nan_to_num(chm)
    return P,S,zland
def render(S,zland,cam,light='sun',tide=0.45,params=None):
    G=gbuffer(cam,zland,sea_level=tide)
    ramp,step,lay=paint_sea(G,S,cam,params)
    ramp,step=paint_land(G,S,cam,ramp,step,tide)
    return to_rgb(ramp,step,light),G
def photo_view(G,Hm,photo,frame=(350,250)):
    im=np.asarray(Image.open(photo).convert('RGB'))
    u,v=apply(Hm,(G['x']+frame[0]).ravel(),(G['y']+frame[1]).ravel())
    u=u.reshape(G['x'].shape); v=v.reshape(G['x'].shape)
    ok=(u>=0)&(u<im.shape[1]-1)&(v>=0)&(v<im.shape[0]-1)&G['sea']
    out=np.zeros(G['x'].shape+(3,),np.uint8); out[ok]=im[v[ok].astype(int),u[ok].astype(int)]
    return out,ok
if __name__=='__main__':
    t=time.time(); P,S,zl=fields()
    Hm=np.load('../notes/H_poa.npy')
    O='~/work/voxsim-data/places/olycoast/obliques/point_of_arches__060819_15405_2016.jpg'
    mpp=float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 1.0
    cam=Cam(cx=720,cy=750,mpp=mpp,W=480,H=300,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
    rgb,G=render(S,zl,cam)
    ph,ok=photo_view(G,Hm,O)
    # photo at pixel size: box-average the photo footprint -> sample at 4x then box down
    cam4=Cam(cx=720,cy=750,mpp=mpp/4,W=480*4,H=300*4,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
    G4=gbuffer(cam4,np.full((10,10),-40.0),sea_level=0.45)
    ph4,_=photo_view(G4,Hm,O)
    ph=np.asarray(Image.fromarray(ph4).resize((480,300),Image.BOX))
    k=3; W,H=480,300
    sheet=Image.new('RGB',(W*k*2+10,H*k+20),(20,20,24)); d=ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
    sheet.paste(Image.fromarray(ph).resize((W*k,H*k),Image.NEAREST),(0,20))
    sheet.paste(Image.fromarray(rgb).resize((W*k,H*k),Image.NEAREST),(W*k+10,20))
    d.text((4,4),f'photo 060819_15405 warped to iso 30deg, box-averaged to {mpp} m/px (sea plane only)',fill=(230,230,230))
    d.text((W*k+14,4),f'mine, same view, same place (lidar), summer swell Hs 1.57 m 8.3 s, sun',fill=(230,230,230))
    out=sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else '../stages/s1_poa_iso.png'
    sheet.save(out); Image.fromarray(rgb).save(out.replace('.png','_1x.png')); print('t',time.time()-t)
EOF

Bash: Add Lagrangian foam particles to sim
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('sea/sim.py').read()
# particles
s=s.replace("""        W = np.zeros((h, w), np.float32); F = np.zeros_like(W); A = np.zeros_like(W)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed)""","""        W = np.zeros((h, w), np.float32); F = np.zeros_like(W); A = np.zeros_like(W)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed)
        # Lagrangian foam: particles shed by decaying whitewater, carried by the current, dying on an
        # exponential clock (tau_f). No numerical diffusion, so streaks stay thin and long.
        px = np.zeros(0, np.float32); py = np.zeros(0, np.float32); pa = np.zeros(0, np.float32); pl = np.zeros(0, np.float32)
        pk = np.zeros(0, np.float32)""")
s=s.replace("""            if i % 2 == 0:
                F = advect(F, self.u, self.v, 2 * dt); A = advect(A, self.u, self.v, 2 * dt)""","""            # shed particles where whitewater is decaying (rate ~ lost foam), jittered inside the cell
            shed = (lost > 0.02) & ~self.land
            ys, xs = np.nonzero(shed)
            if ys.size:
                pr = np.clip(lost[ys, xs] * P.get('shed', 2.5), 0, 1)
                keep = rng.random(ys.size) < pr
                ys, xs = ys[keep], xs[keep]
                px = np.concatenate([px, xs + rng.random(xs.size).astype(np.float32)])
                py = np.concatenate([py, ys + rng.random(ys.size).astype(np.float32)])
                pa = np.concatenate([pa, np.zeros(xs.size, np.float32)])
                pl = np.concatenate([pl, rng.exponential(P['tau_f'], xs.size).astype(np.float32)])
                pk = np.concatenate([pk, rng.random(xs.size).astype(np.float32)])
            if px.size:
                from .waves import bilinear as _bl
                uu = _bl(self.u, px, py); vv = _bl(self.v, px, py)
                sd = np.sqrt(2 * P.get('diff', 0.02) * dt)
                px = px + uu * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size); py = py + vv * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size)
                pa = pa + dt
                ok = (pa < pl) & (px >= 0) & (px < w - 1) & (py >= 0) & (py < h - 1)
                ok &= ~self.land[np.clip(py.astype(int), 0, h - 1), np.clip(px.astype(int), 0, w - 1)]
                px, py, pa, pl, pk = px[ok], py[ok], pa[ok], pl[ok], pk[ok]
            if i % 2 == 0:
                A = advect(A, self.u, self.v, 2 * dt)""")
s=s.replace("""        self.W, self.F, self.A = W, np.clip(F, 0, 3), A""","""        self.W, self.A = W, A
        self.parts = dict(x=px, y=py, age=pa, life=pl, k=pk)
        # foam density on the 1 m grid from the particles (for coarse views and stats)
        Fd = np.zeros((h, w), np.float32)
        np.add.at(Fd, (py.astype(int), px.astype(int)), 1.0)
        self.F = np.clip(nd.gaussian_filter(Fd, 0.7) * P.get('part_gain', 0.5), 0, 3)""")
open('sea/sim.py','w').write(s)
t=open('t_sim.py').read()
t=t.replace("np.savez_compressed('../cache/poa_state.npz',","np.savez_compressed('../cache/poa_parts.npz',**S.parts)\nnp.savez_compressed('../cache/poa_state.npz',")
open('t_sim.py','w').write(t)
EOF

s=open('sea/sim.py').read()
s=s.replace("""        u, v = ambient_current(self.land, current)""","""        u, v = ambient_current(self.land, current)
        # eddies: a divergence-free meander field (curl of a smooth stream function), ~0.1 m/s at 100-200 m.
        # Without it foam streaks are straight rulers; the photos' streaks swing in S-curves.
        psi = fbm((h, w), 160, seed + 21, octaves=2)
        gyp, gxp = np.gradient(psi)
        ue, ve = gyp, -gxp
        sc = eddy / (np.percentile(np.hypot(ue, ve), 90) + 1e-9)
        dl0 = nd.distance_transform_edt(~self.land)
        fade = np.clip(dl0 / 15.0, 0, 1)
        u = u + ue * sc * fade; v = v + ve * sc * fade""")

"""Lockstep: the same place, fields and palette at 0.1, 0.3, 1 and 3 m/px, each beside the photo
warped into the same view (box-averaged to the same pixel size)."""
import sys, time; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from stage1 import fields, render, photo_view
from sea.render import Cam, gbuffer
O='~/work/voxsim-data/places/olycoast/obliques/point_of_arches__060819_15405_2016.jpg'
def photo_at(Hm,cx,cy,mpp,W,H,ss=4):
    c4=Cam(cx=cx,cy=cy,mpp=mpp/ss,W=W*ss,H=H*ss,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
    G4=gbuffer(c4,np.full((10,10),-40.0),sea_level=0.45)
    ph4,_=photo_view(G4,Hm,O)
    return np.asarray(Image.fromarray(ph4).resize((W,H),Image.BOX))
if __name__=='__main__':
    P,S,zl=fields(); S['parts']=dict(np.load('../cache/poa_parts.npz'))
    Hm=np.load('../notes/H_poa.npy')
    cx,cy=float(sys.argv[1]),float(sys.argv[2]); out=sys.argv[3]
    scales=[0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]; W,H=240,150; k=3
    sheet=Image.new('RGB',(2*W*k+10,len(scales)*(H*k+16)),(20,20,24)); d=ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
    for i,m in enumerate(scales):
        cam=Cam(cx=cx,cy=cy,mpp=m,W=W,H=H,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
        rgb,G=render(S,zl,cam)
        ph=photo_at(Hm,cx,cy,m,W,H)
        y=i*(H*k+16)
        d.text((4,y+2),f'photo, {m} m/px',fill=(220,220,220)); d.text((W*k+14,y+2),f'mine, {m} m/px',fill=(220,220,220))
        sheet.paste(Image.fromarray(ph).resize((W*k,H*k),Image.NEAREST),(0,y+16))
        sheet.paste(Image.fromarray(rgb).resize((W*k,H*k),Image.NEAREST),(W*k+10,y+16))
    sheet.save(out)
EOF

# output:
# voxsim infrastructure: where work runs, where things live

For every lane. Read this once. Everything here is meant to be **easy and fast**. If any step takes more than about 10 minutes of fiddling, stop and say so in your NOTES and your report, and ask for help. Don't grind on infrastructure.

## Credentials

- `~/work/.env.local` holds Modal and AWS keys. Load them with `set -a; . ~/work/.env.local; set +a`.
- **Never print, echo, log or commit their values.**

## Where compute runs
| Work | Where |
|---|---|
| Reading, writing code, small checks, `cargo check` on one crate | Your worktree, locally. `.cargo/config.toml` caps builds at 2 jobs. |
| Release builds of the workspace, long sims or soaks, render sweeps, heavy numpy | **Modal** (below). The laptop crashed from memory pressure with 14 lanes building at once, so don't run more than one heavy local job at a time. |
| Self-contained work that only needs the repo | Cloud agents; the manager decides. |

## Modal

- **Workspace:** set `MODAL_ENVIRONMENT=main` after loading the env file (the local `~/.modal.toml` defaults to another workspace's environment). A probe container with 16 CPUs came up in about 6 s.
- **Image:** use `modal.Image.from_registry("rust:1-bookworm", add_python="3.12")`. It ships current Rust. `debian_slim().apt_install(...)` failed on a stale Debian security mirror, so don't use it for Rust.
- **Budget:** the whole project has about $30 of Modal credit for now.
  - Size containers to the job, and set `timeout=`.
  - Shut down what you start (no idle `keep_warm`).
  - Log each Modal run's resources and wall time in your NOTES, so spend can be tracked.
  - If a job will take more than ~1 CPU-hour, say so before you run it.
- **Volumes:** use them for joint disk-and-CPU work (a cargo target cache per lane, shared data). Name everything `voxsim-<lane>-<what>` (e.g. `voxsim-place-yakima-target`), so nothing sprawls untracked.

Bash: Write Modal runner for sims
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat > modal_sim.py <<'EOF'
"""Run swell + sea-state sims on Modal (the laptop's load average is >100 with ~30 lanes).
Usage (local): python3 modal_sim.py jobs.json   -- each job: name, depth npz path, params. Results -> ../cache/<name>.npz"""
import modal, io, json, sys, os
import numpy as np
app = modal.App("voxsim-study-sea-sim")
image = (modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.12").pip_install("numpy==1.26.4", "scipy==1.14.0")
         .add_local_dir(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "sea"), "/root/sea"))

@app.function(image=image, cpu=2.0, memory=8192, timeout=3600)
def run(blob: bytes, params: dict) -> bytes:
    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "/root")
    import numpy as np, io, time
    from sea.waves import swell, eikonal
    from sea.sim import SeaState
    t0 = time.time()
    d = dict(np.load(io.BytesIO(blob)))
    depth, z = d['depth'], d['z']
    wp = params.get('waves', {}); sp = params.get('sim', {})
    F = swell(depth, **wp)
    te, kx, ky = eikonal(depth, T=wp.get('T', 8.3), dir_from=wp.get('dir_from', 281.0))
    F['t'] = te; F['kx'] = kx; F['ky'] = ky
    S = SeaState(depth, z, F, wp.get('T', 8.3), **sp).run()
    out = dict(H=F['H'], Q=F['Q'], t=te, kx=kx, ky=ky, land=F['land'],
               W=S.W, F=S.F, A=S.A, q=S.q, Hinst=S.Hinst, brk=S.brk, kelp=S.kelp, kelp_rho=S.kelp_rho,
               rock=S.rock, rough=S.rough, u=S.u, v=S.v,
               px=S.parts['x'], py=S.parts['y'], page=S.parts['age'], plife=S.parts['life'], pk=S.parts['k'],
               t_now=np.array(S.t), secs=np.array(time.time() - t0))
    b = io.BytesIO(); np.savez_compressed(b, **{k: np.asarray(v) for k, v in out.items()}); return b.getvalue()

@app.local_entrypoint()
def main(jobs: str):
    J = json.load(open(jobs))
    args = []
    for j in J:
        args.append((open(j['input'], 'rb').read(), j['params']))
    for j, res in zip(J, run.starmap(args)):
        open(j['output'], 'wb').write(res)
        print('done', j['output'], len(res))
EOF
echo ok

ok

Bash: Add perspective eye-level G-buffer
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat >> sea/render.py <<'EOF'

class PerspCam:
    """Eye-level camera for the shore view. pos in grid coords (x, y) + eye height z (m, absolute);
    heading = bearing (deg clockwise from north) the camera faces; pitch (deg, negative = down); hfov deg."""
    def __init__(self, x, y, z, heading, pitch=-2.0, hfov=60.0, W=480, H=270):
        self.x, self.y, self.z, self.heading, self.pitch, self.hfov, self.W, self.H = x, y, z, heading, pitch, hfov, W, H
        self.mpp = None

def persp_gbuffer(cam, zgrid, sea_level=0.0, max_dist=4000.0, step0=0.25):
    W, H = cam.W, cam.H
    f = (W / 2) / np.tan(np.radians(cam.hfov / 2))
    py, px = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W].astype(np.float64) + 0.5
    # camera basis (grid coords: x east, y south, z up)
    b = np.radians(cam.heading); p = np.radians(cam.pitch)
    fwd = np.array([np.sin(b) * np.cos(p), -np.cos(b) * np.cos(p), np.sin(p)])
    right = np.array([np.cos(b), np.sin(b), 0.0])
    up = np.cross(right, fwd)
    d = fwd[None, None] * f + right[None, None] * (px - W / 2)[..., None] - up[None, None] * (py - H / 2)[..., None]
    d /= np.linalg.norm(d, axis=2, keepdims=True)
    dx, dy, dz = d[..., 0], d[..., 1], d[..., 2]
    # sea plane
    tsea = np.where(dz < -1e-6, (cam.z - sea_level) / np.maximum(-dz, 1e-9), np.inf)
    tsea = np.minimum(tsea, max_dist)
    # march terrain (geometric steps) until tsea
    zz = np.nan_to_num(zgrid, nan=-50.0); h, w = zz.shape
    thit = np.full((H, W), np.inf)
    t = np.full((H, W), 0.5); prev_below = np.zeros((H, W), bool)
    for i in range(400):
        x = cam.x + dx * t; y = cam.y + dy * t; zr = cam.z + dz * t
        inside = (x >= 0) & (x < w - 1) & (y >= 0) & (y < h - 1)
        zt = np.where(inside, bilinear(zz, x, y), -50)
        hit = (zt > zr) & np.isinf(thit) & (t < tsea)
        thit = np.where(hit, t, thit)
        t = t + np.maximum(step0, t * 0.01)
        if (t > np.minimum(tsea, max_dist)).all(): break
    sea = np.isfinite(tsea) & (tsea < thit) & (tsea < max_dist)
    land = np.isfinite(thit) & ~sea
    tt = np.where(sea, tsea, np.where(land, thit, np.nan))
    G = dict(x=cam.x + dx * np.nan_to_num(tt), y=cam.y + dy * np.nan_to_num(tt), z=cam.z + dz * np.nan_to_num(tt),
             sea=sea, land=land, sky=~sea & ~land, dist=tt, dz=dz, dirx=dx, diry=dy)
    # pixel footprint on the ground (m): distance / f, stretched vertically by 1/sin(grazing)
    G['mpp'] = np.nan_to_num(tt, nan=1e3) / f
    G['graze'] = np.degrees(np.arcsin(np.clip(-dz, 1e-4, 1)))
    gyz, gxz = np.gradient(zz)
    nx = -bilinear(gxz, G['x'], G['y']); ny = -bilinear(gyz, G['x'], G['y']); n = np.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny + 1)
    G['nx'], G['ny'], G['nz'] = nx / n, ny / n, 1 / n
    return G
EOF
echo ok; cat ../cache/sim.log

Bash: Scene loader for Modal results; hook drivers
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat > sea/scene.py <<'EOF'
"""Load a sim result (Modal npz) + place into the painter's field dict."""
import numpy as np, os, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from .place import PLACES, load
from dem import window
CACHE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))), 'cache')
def scene(place, result, tide=0.45, T=None):
    P = load(place, tide)
    d = dict(np.load(os.path.join(CACHE, result) if not os.path.isabs(result) else result))
    pack, X0, Y1, (c0, r0, c1, r1) = PLACES[place]
    chm = np.nan_to_num(window(pack, 'chm', X0 + c0, Y1 - r1, X0 + c1, Y1 - r0, dtype=np.uint8))
    f = lambda k: d[k].astype(np.float32)
    S = dict(depth=np.nan_to_num(P['depth'], nan=30.0), rock=f('rock'), rough=f('rough'), kelp=f('kelp'),
             kelp_rho=f('kelp_rho'), W=f('W'), F=f('F'), A=f('A'), Hinst=f('Hinst'), u=f('u'), v=f('v'),
             tt=d['t'].astype(np.float32), brkf=d['brk'].astype(np.float32), T=float(T or 8.3),
             t_now=float(d['t_now']), chm=chm, H=f('H'), kx=f('kx'), ky=f('ky'),
             parts=dict(x=d['px'], y=d['py']))
    zland = np.nan_to_num(P['z'], nan=-40) + chm
    return P, S, zland
EOF

Bash: Append simulation rules to LESSONS
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea; cat >> LESSONS.md <<'EOF'

### 1.4 The rules a simulation needs (numbers, with where they come from)

**Swell and refraction** (`src/sea/waves.py`)

- Linear dispersion ω² = g k tanh(k d); phase speed c = ω/k, group speed c_g = n c with
  n = ½(1 + 2kd / sinh 2kd) **[source: SPM ch. 2]**. Deep-water wavelength L0 = 1.56 T²: summer 8 s → 100 m,
  winter 13 s → 260 m.
- Olympic wave climate (NDBC 46041, measured by the water study): Hs median 1.1 m (Jul) to 3.0 m (Jan);
  Tp 8 s (Jul) to 13 s (Jan); storms 8–10 m.
- Crests bend toward shallow water (Snell): I trace 2,600 rays across the lidar at 0.5 m steps with
  dk̂/ds = −(1/c)(∇c − (∇c·k̂)k̂). Height H = H0·K_s·K_r·(dissipation), K_s = sqrt(c_g0/c_g),
  K_r = sqrt(b0/b) from the spacing of *living* neighbour rays (a ray that hits a rock freezes; counting it
  as a neighbour faked a 5× spreading — my bug, now fixed).
- Crest *positions* come from a separate first-arrival travel-time solve (Dijkstra, 16 neighbours, 2 m),
  because rays leave shadows behind stacks; the eikonal wraps crests into every lee **[measured on my
  solve: `notes/poa_waves.png`]**.
- **Coves get small waves, headlands big ones** — rays diverge over the cove and converge on the points.
  At Point of the Arches, Hrms falls from 1.08 m offshore to 0.47 m inside the cove with no breaking at all
  **[measured on my solve]**. This is SPM's textbook figure, and it's why the coast painter's uniform
  "surf band around every shore" reads false.
- Random-wave breaking (Battjes & Janssen 1978): fraction breaking Q_b from (1−Q)/ln Q = −(Hrms/Hm)²,
  Hm = 0.88/k·tanh(γkd/0.88), γ ≈ 0.73; dissipation D = ¼αρg f Q_b Hm².
- **Which crest breaks where** (the picture needs individuals, not averages): each crest gets a height
  multiplier from a Rayleigh draw that varies slowly along the crest (30 m cells) and is fixed for that crest
  (hashed on crest number). It breaks where H_inst > 0.62·d_pinnacle, where d_pinnacle is the shallowest
  depth within 5 m (the 1 m lidar blurs reef heads that waves feel).

**Foam** (`src/sea/sim.py`), three quantities integrated over the last 60 wave periods (~8 min):

- **W, whitewater**: set to 1 on a crest the moment it breaks, decays with τ ≈ 8 s. Because the source
  moves with the crest and the old foam stays behind, every break draws itself as a crescent with a sharp
  shoreward edge and a soft seaward tail, the shape in `notes/poa_streak.png`. Nothing places a crescent.
- **Surge collar**: every crest that reaches within ~2 m of rock throws whitewater whatever the depth rule
  says, weighted by how squarely it faces the rock.
- **A, aeration** (the jade halo), τ ≈ 14 s, advected, extended ~6 m around stacks.
- **F, persistent foam, as particles**: decaying whitewater sheds particles, carried by the current and
  killed on an exponential clock. Two populations: 88% short-lived (τ 25 s), 12% long-lived (τ 450 s,
  surfactant foam). An Eulerian foam field smeared the streaks into fog after a few hundred advection steps;
  particles keep them thin.
- **Current**: a uniform alongshore drift (0.25 m/s) steered around the land by potential flow, plus a
  divergence-free eddy field (0.12 m/s at 160 m scale) so streaks swing into S-curves, plus 3% of the wind,
  Stokes drift, and a 0.35 m/s shoreward push inside breaking zones.

**Kelp** (bull kelp, *Nereocystis*): on rock, 2–18 m deep, densest in the moderately exposed middle depths;
beds tens of metres across with gaps; within a bed, a mat whose area fraction equals the expected canopy
cover ρ; beyond the mat, floats as specks streaming down-current. Floats 9–15 cm, 30–64 blades to 4–10 m
**[source: water study 1.7]**; canopy area falls 22–36% per metre of tide rise **[source: Timmer et al.
2024]**. WA's outer coast plus Strait held ~1,700 ha of floating kelp in 2005; DNR has flown annual canopy
surveys since 1989 **[source: WA DNR Nearshore Habitat Program]**.
EOF
echo ok

Bash: Foam as coverage with noise-contour edges; render lockstep
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; python3 - <<'EOF'
p=open('sea/paint.py').read()
a=p.index("    # ---------------- 6. subsided foam")
b=p.index("    # ---------------- 8. the roller")
p=p[:a]+"""    # ---------------- 6+7. foam as COVERAGE: solid shapes with ragged, noise-contour edges ----------
    # (no per-pixel stipple: the threshold field is smooth at >= 3 px, so edges are ragged, never salt)
    un = np.hypot(u, v) + 1e-6
    Fe = np.clip(Ff, 0, 2)
    cov = np.maximum(np.clip(Wf * 1.25, 0, 1.2), Fe)
    ecell = cq(1.6, mpp, 3.0)
    # threshold noise stretched along the current: patches and streaks elongate the way the foam drifts
    xa = (x * (u / un) + y * (v / un)) / P['lace_stretch']; ya = -x * (v / un) + y * (u / un)
    thr = 0.18 + 0.62 * fbmw(xa, ya, ecell, 68, 2)
    foam = sea & (cov > thr)
    # Ruskin's oval gaps / Gilland's expanding holes: only where the holes are >= 4 px across
    hcell = cq(P['lace_cell'], mpp, 4.0)
    hf1, hf2 = worley(x, y, hcell, 66, u / un, v / un, 1.6)
    hole_r = np.clip(0.75 - cov * 0.7, 0, 0.7)                  # older, thinner foam -> bigger holes
    holes = foam & (hf1 < hole_r) & (cov < 0.85) & (hcell / np.asarray(mpp) >= 4.0)
    foam &= ~holes
    ramp[foam] = RID['foam']
    # tone: fresh whitewater gets lumps lit toward the sun and shaded away; old foam is the mid tone,
    # its densest cores lit
    lcell = cq(P['lump_cell'], mpp, 2.0)
    lump = fbmw(x, y, lcell, 77, 3)
    saz = np.radians(P['sun_az'])
    sx_, sy_ = np.sin(saz), -np.cos(saz)
    e = 0.35 * lcell
    lg = fbmw(x + sx_ * e, y + sy_ * e, lcell, 77, 3) - lump
    fresh = Wf > 0.35
    tone = np.where(fresh, np.where(lg > 0.02, 3, np.where(lg < -0.035, 1, 2)), np.where(cov > 0.75, 2, 1))
    step[foam] = tone[foam]
"""+p[b:]
open('sea/paint.py','w').write(p)
s=open('sea/sim.py').read()
s=s.replace("self.collar = (np.exp(-dl / 1.8) * (0.25 + 0.75 * facing) * (dl < 6)).astype(np.float32)","self.collar = (np.exp(-dl / 1.8) * (0.05 + 0.95 * facing) * (dl < 6)).astype(np.float32)")
s=s.replace("ca = np.clip(np.exp(-self.dl / 6.0) * (0.3 + 0.7 * (self.collar > 0.05))","ca = np.clip(np.exp(-self.dl / 9.0) * (0.3 + 0.7 * (self.collar > 0.05))")
open('sea/sim.py','w').write(s)
EOF
nice python3 lockstep.py 720 750 ../stages/s1_poa_lockstep_v2.png

Bash: Write shore eye-level view driver
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; cat > shore.py <<'EOF'
"""Eye-level view from the beach, looking out to sea (perspective)."""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from sea.scene import scene
from sea.render import PerspCam, persp_gbuffer
from sea.paint import paint_sea, paint_land, bayer
from sea.pal import to_rgb, RID
def find_stand(z, x0, y0, r=80):
    best=None
    for yy in range(int(y0-r),int(y0+r)):
        for xx in range(int(x0-r),int(x0+r)):
            v=z[yy,xx]
            if 1.5<v<3.0:
                dd=(xx-x0)**2+(yy-y0)**2
                if best is None or dd<best[0]: best=(dd,xx,yy,v)
    return best
def render_shore(S,zl,cam,light='sun',tide=0.45):
    G=persp_gbuffer(cam,zl,sea_level=tide)
    class C: pass
    c=C(); c.mpp=G['mpp']
    ramp,step,lay=paint_sea(G,S,c,dict(sun_az=205))
    G2=dict(G); G2['sea']=~G['land']            # paint_land paints where ~sea
    ramp,step=paint_land(G2,S,c,ramp,step,tide)
    sky=G['sky']; H,W=sky.shape
    # sky: flat bands by elevation with a checker seam (the sky study owns the real sky)
    el=np.degrees(np.arcsin(np.clip(np.nan_to_num(G['dz']),-1,1)))
    band=np.clip((el/12.0*6).astype(int),0,5)
    ramp[sky]=RID['sky']; step[sky]=(5-band)[sky]
    return to_rgb(ramp,step,light),G
if __name__=='__main__':
    place=sys.argv[1]; res=sys.argv[2]; px,py,heading=map(float,sys.argv[3:6]); out=sys.argv[6]
    light=sys.argv[7] if len(sys.argv)>7 else 'sun'
    P,S,zl=scene(place,res)
    z=np.nan_to_num(P['z'],nan=-40)
    b=find_stand(z,px,py); print('stand',b)
    cam=PerspCam(b[1],b[2],b[3]+1.7,heading,pitch=-3.0,hfov=55,W=480,H=270)
    rgb,G=render_shore(S,zl,cam,light)
    Image.fromarray(rgb).resize((1440,810),Image.NEAREST).save(out)
EOF
echo ok

# output:
ok

Bash: Add wind whitecaps and windrows to sim
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('sea/sim.py').read()
# whitecaps in the step loop, before decay block uses W
s=s.replace("""            src = brk & passed
""","""            src = brk & passed
            if self.wc_cov > 0:
                W = np.maximum(W, self.whitecaps(rng, dt))
""")
s=s.replace("""    def crest_break(self, t):""","""    def whitecaps(self, rng, dt):
        \"\"\"wind-sea breaking in deep water (Beaufort 4+). Coverage from Monahan & O'Muircheartaigh (1980):
        W = 3.84e-6 U^3.41 (U = 10 m wind, m/s): 5 m/s -> 0.09%, 10 m/s -> 1%, 17 m/s -> 6%, 22 m/s -> 14%.
        Each whitecap is a short dash along the wind, 2-8 m, lasting ~tau_w.\"\"\"
        h, w = self.d.shape
        P = self.params
        # steady coverage = rate * tau * area: rate per m^2 per s
        area = 6.0
        rate = self.wc_cov / (P['tau_w'] * area)
        n = rng.poisson(rate * dt * h * w)
        out = np.zeros((h, w), np.float32)
        if n == 0: return out
        x0 = rng.random(n) * w; y0 = rng.random(n) * h
        L = rng.uniform(2, 8, n)
        for f in np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, 7):
            xs = np.clip((x0 + f * L * self.wdir[0]).astype(int), 0, w - 1)
            ys = np.clip((y0 + f * L * self.wdir[1]).astype(int), 0, h - 1)
            out[ys, xs] = 1.0
        out = np.clip(nd.gaussian_filter(out, 0.6) * 3.0, 0, 1)
        return np.where((self.d > 3) & ~self.land, out, 0)

    def crest_break(self, t):""")
# init: wind params
s=s.replace("""        self.params = dict(tau_w=tau_w,""","""        U = float(np.hypot(*wind)); self.U = U
        self.wdir = (wind[0] / (U + 1e-9), wind[1] / (U + 1e-9))
        self.wc_cov = 3.84e-6 * U ** 3.41 if U > 3 else 0.0
        self.params = dict(tau_w=tau_w,""")
# windrows in particle advection
s=s.replace("""                px = px + uu * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size); py = py + vv * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size)""","""                if self.U > 5:
                    # Langmuir windrows: surface convergence into lines along the wind, spacing ~ 25 m
                    nxp, nyp = -self.wdir[1], self.wdir[0]
                    sperp = px * nxp + py * nyp
                    vc = -0.004 * self.U * np.sin(2 * np.pi * sperp / P.get('windrow', 25.0))
                    uu = uu + vc * nxp; vv = vv + vc * nyp
                px = px + uu * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size); py = py + vv * dt + rng.normal(0, sd, px.size)""")
open('sea/sim.py','w').write(s)
EOF

Bash: Add Cox-Munk sun glitter to painter
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; python3 - <<'EOF'
p=open('sea/paint.py').read()
p=p.replace("    return ramp, step, dict(q=q,","""    # ---------------- 9. sun glitter (Cox & Munk 1954): facets tilted to mirror the sun into the eye --------
    if P.get('sun_el', 50) > -1 and P.get('glitter', 1.0) > 0:
        sel, saz = np.radians(P['sun_el']), np.radians(P['sun_az'])
        sv = np.array([np.sin(saz) * np.cos(sel), -np.cos(saz) * np.cos(sel), np.sin(sel)])
        if 'dirx' in G:
            dxv, dyv, dzv = G['dirx'], G['diry'], G['dz']
        else:
            e = np.radians(cam.elev); fx, fy = -cam.V[0], -cam.V[1]
            dxv = np.full(x.shape, fx * np.cos(e)); dyv = np.full(x.shape, fy * np.cos(e)); dzv = np.full(x.shape, -np.sin(e))
        hx, hy, hz = sv[0] - dxv, sv[1] - dyv, sv[2] - dzv
        hn = np.sqrt(hx * hx + hy * hy + hz * hz) + 1e-9
        cth = np.clip(hz / hn, 1e-3, 1); tan2 = (1 - cth * cth) / (cth * cth)
        U = P.get('wind_speed', 3.0); s2 = 0.003 + 0.00512 * U
        pg = np.exp(-tan2 / s2) * P.get('glitter', 1.0)
        gcell = cq(0.8, mpp, 1.0)
        hsh = hash2(np.floor(x / gcell), np.floor(y / gcell * 2.5), 123)
        gl = sea & (hsh < pg * 0.35) & ~(ramp == RID['foam'])
        ramp[gl] = RID['glint']; step[gl] = 0
        gl2 = sea & (hsh < pg * 0.8) & ~gl & ~(ramp == RID['foam']) & (ramp != RID['kelp'])
        step[gl2] = np.minimum(step[gl2] + 2, 6)
    return ramp, step, dict(q=q,""")
open('sea/paint.py','w').write(p)
EOF

s=open('sea/scene.py').read()
s=s.replace("    zland = np.nan_to_num","""    S['eta'] = eta_field(S)
    zland = np.nan_to_num""")
s+='''
def eta_field(S):
    """sea-surface elevation now (m, about still water), 1 m grid: each crest's own height, a Stokes-sharpened
    profile, flattened where broken (bores are lower and flat-topped)."""
    T = S['T']; q = np.mod((S['t_now'] - S['tt']) / T, 1.0)
    a = 0.5 * S['Hinst']
    ph = 2 * np.pi * q
    prof = np.cos(ph) + 0.35 * (np.cos(2 * ph) - 0.3)             # peaked crests, long flat troughs
    brk = S['brkf'] > 0.5
    prof = np.where(brk, np.clip(prof, -1, 0.6), prof)
    d = S['depth']
    return (a * prof * np.clip(d / 0.8, 0, 1)).astype(np.float32)
'''
open('sea/scene.py','w').write(s)

        if (t > np.minimum(np.minimum(tsea, thit), max_dist)).all(): break
    if eta is not None:
        # refine every hit by bisection (kills the stair-step columns of a fixed-step march)
        th = np.minimum(tsea, thit); fin = np.isfinite(th)
        hi = np.where(fin, th, 0); lo = np.where(fin, np.maximum(hi - np.maximum(step0, hi * 0.01), 0.1), 0)
        for _ in range(7):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            x = cam.x + dx * mid; y = cam.y + dy * mid; zr = cam.z + dz * mid
            zt = bilinear(zw, x, y)
            above = zt > zr
            hi = np.where(fin & above, mid, hi); lo = np.where(fin & ~above, mid, lo)
        tsea = np.where(np.isfinite(tsea), hi, tsea); thit = np.where(np.isfinite(thit), hi, thit)""")

import json
states={'calm':dict(w=dict(Hs=0.6,T=7.0,dir_from=275),s=dict(wind=[0.0,0.0],current=[0.05,-0.15],kelp_amount=1.0)),
        'swell':dict(w=dict(Hs=1.6,T=8.3,dir_from=281),s=dict(wind=[1.0,2.0],current=[0.05,-0.25],kelp_amount=0.8)),
        'storm':dict(w=dict(Hs=5.5,T=14.0,dir_from=250),s=dict(wind=[-10.0,-15.0],current=[-0.1,-0.4],kelp_amount=0.1))}

J=[]
for p,km in places.items():
    for st,c in states.items():
        w=dict(c['w'],smooth=8.0); s=dict(c['s'],n_periods=60 if st!='storm' else 40,steps_per_T=8,tide=0.45,long_frac=0.3,shed=7.5)
        s['kelp_amount']=s['kelp_amount']*km

T = {'calm':7.0,'swell':8.3,'storm':14.0,'summer':8.3}
WIND = {'calm':1.0,'swell':3.0,'storm':18.0,'summer':3.0}
ISO_C = {'poa':(720,750),'alava':(430,700),'kala':(380,800)}
STAND = {'poa':(1020,760,270),'alava':(470,760,265),'kala':(470,820,265)}
_cache = {}
def get(place, state):
    k=(place,state)
    if k not in _cache:
        _cache.clear(); _cache[k]=scene(place,f'{place}_{state}.npz',T=T[state])
    return _cache[k]
def params(light, state):
    L=LIGHTS[light]
    return dict(sun_az=L['sun_az'], sun_el=L['sun_el'], glitter=1.0 if L['direct']>0 else 0.0, wind_speed=WIND[state])
def panel(place, state, light, view, W=240, H=150):
    P,S,zl=get(place,state)
    if view.startswith('iso'):
        mpp=float(view[3:]); cx,cy=ISO_C[place]
        cam=Cam(cx=cx,cy=cy,mpp=mpp,W=W,H=H,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
        rgb,G=render(S,zl,cam,light=light,params=params(light,state))
    else:
        x,y,hd=STAND[place]; z=np.nan_to_num(P['z'],nan=-40); b=find_stand(z,x,y)
        cam=PerspCam(b[1],b[2],b[3]+1.7,hd,pitch=-3.0,hfov=55,W=W,H=H)
        rgb,G=render_shore(S,zl,cam,light)
    return rgb
def sheet(rows, cols, cellfn, rlab, clab, title, out, k=2, W=240, H=150):
    lw=110; th=24
    im=Image.new('RGB',(lw+len(cols)*(W*k+6),th+len(rows)*(H*k+18)),(18,18,22)); d=ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((6,6),title,fill=(235,235,235))
    for j,c in enumerate(cols): d.text((lw+j*(W*k+6)+4,th-2),clab(c),fill=(200,200,200))
    for i,r in enumerate(rows):
        y=th+10+i*(H*k+18); d.text((4,y+H*k//2),rlab(r),fill=(220,220,220))
        for j,c in enumerate(cols):
            rgb=cellfn(r,c)
            im.paste(Image.fromarray(rgb).resize((W*k,H*k),Image.NEAREST),(lw+j*(W*k+6),y))
        im.save(out)
    im.save(out)
if __name__=='__main__':
    which=sys.argv[1]
    if which=='A':   # sea state x place (depth/bottom) in the iso 1 m/px view, sun
        places=['kala','poa','alava']; names={'kala':'Kalaloch: sand','poa':'P. of Arches: rock reef','alava':'Cape Alava: kelp'}
        states=['calm','swell','storm']
        for view in ['iso1','iso3','shore']:
            order=[(p,s) for p in places for s in states]
            sheet(states, places, lambda s,p: panel(p,s,'sun',view), lambda s:s, lambda p:names[p],
                  f'Sheet A: sea state x bottom, view {view}, sun (rows: calm Hs 0.6 m/7 s, swell 1.6 m/8.3 s, storm 5.5 m/14 s + 18 m/s wind)',
                  f'../sheets/A_state_x_bottom_{view}.png')
    if which=='B':   # light x view at swell
        lights=['sun','overcast','golden','dusk','fog']; views=['iso1','iso3','shore']
        for place in sys.argv[2:] or ['poa']:
            sheet(lights, views, lambda l,v: panel(place,'swell',l,v), lambda l:l, lambda v:v,
                  f'Sheet B: weather and hour x view, {place}, swell (same index canvas, palette relit)', f'../sheets/B_light_x_view_{place}.png')
EOF
mkdir -p ../sheets; echo ok

cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea; cat >> LESSONS.md <<'EOF'

### 1.5 Light, weather and hour (how the sea's colour moves)

The water pixel is body + mirror (the water study's rule 1.1, which I keep): `colour = body × irradiance ×
sun tint + F_eff × sky`. I calibrate the *body* from the sunny photos (1.2) by subtracting the sunny sky's
reflection, then relight (`src/sea/pal.py`):

| state | irradiance (vs. sunny afternoon) | F_eff | what happens |
|---|---|---|---|
| sun, 14:30 summer | 1.0 | 0.06 | depth and bottom read strongly; foam blue-white with lit/shaded lumps |
| overcast | 0.40 | 0.09 | the grey sky mirror dominates; depth bands shrink to 2–3 values; foam is only a little above the water and has no lit side |
| golden (sun 9°) | 0.42, warm | 0.07 | foam lit sides turn peach, shadow sides take the sky; water loses its green |
| dusk (sun −2°) | 0.10 | 0.09 | body gone: the sea is the sky's mirror, violet-grey; foam is barely lighter than water |
| fog / marine layer | 0.45 + veil 45% | 0.10 | a flat pale field; only near foam and kelp keep value |

Photographic facts behind the table: the 10:40 morning 2002 obliques (sun low in the east-southeast,
camera looking west) have no colour at all over the cove — a silver sheet of glitter; under the August
2016 afternoon sun the same cove is saturated teal. Sun glitter follows Cox & Munk (1954): facet slope
variance σ² = 0.003 + 0.00512·U (U wind at 12.5 m, m/s); a pixel glints when the facet that mirrors the sun
into the eye is within ~1σ of the mean.

### 1.6 Wind sea: whitecaps and windrows

- Whitecap coverage (Monahan & O'Muircheartaigh 1980): W = 3.84×10⁻⁶ U^3.41 — 0.1% at 5 m/s, 1% at 10 m/s,
  6% at 17 m/s, 14% at 22 m/s. Beaufort 4 (5.5–8 m/s) is "fairly frequent white horses"; 6 (11–14 m/s)
  "white foam crests everywhere"; 8 (17–21 m/s) "foam blown in well-marked streaks along the wind".
- Windrows (Langmuir circulation): foam and kelp wrack collect in lines parallel to the wind, spaced
  roughly 10–50 m, once the wind exceeds ~3–5 m/s. In the sim, a transverse convergence velocity
  0.004·U·sin(2πs/25 m) herds the foam particles into them.

### 1.7 What differs in the San Juans (for that lane; not yet painted)

No ocean swell reaches the inner islands; the sea state is wind chop over 5–30 km fetches and tidal current
(Cattle Pass 3–5 kt). The foam vocabulary is the water study's tide rips: convergence lines, boils,
eddy lines, not surf. Kelp beds sit off points and bend with the current (canopy shrinks 35%/m of tide
rise in current). Colour: deeper channels (100–300 m) so darker, bluer water; turbid green after spring
freshets. The obliques at `voxsim-data/places/sanjuan/obliques` (667 frames) show mostly smooth water with
current lines and kelp; a lane could run my sim with current only (Hs ≈ 0) and a tidal current field.
EOF
echo ok

cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea/src; sed -i '' "s/    km = sea \& (kn > P\['kelp_on'\])$/    km = sea \& (kn > P['kelp_on']) \& (krho0 > P.get('mat_rho', 0.22))/" sea/paint.py; grep -n "km = sea" sea/paint.py; cat > sheetC.py <<'EOF'
"""Sheet C: the surf vocabulary around a headland and a stack (Point of the Arches, swell, sun).
Top-left: the physics map (wave height, breaking, crest lines). Others: labelled crops at 0.3 and 1 m/px."""
import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'.')
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from sheets import get, params
from sea.render import Cam
from stage1 import render
P,S,zl=get('poa','swell')
d=np.load('../cache/poa_swell.npz')
H=d['H'].astype(float); Q=d['Q'].astype(float); L=d['land']; tt=d['t'].astype(float)
# physics map, crop around the cove
sl=(slice(300,1100),slice(250,1150))
rgb=np.zeros(H[sl].shape+(3,))
rgb[...,2]=0.25+0.6*np.clip(H[sl]/1.3,0,1); rgb[...,1]=0.15+0.3*np.clip(H[sl]/1.3,0,1)
rgb[...,0]=np.clip(Q[sl]*3,0,1)
ph=np.mod(tt[sl]/8.3,1.0); crest=(ph<0.05)&~L[sl]; rgb[crest]=[1,1,1]
rgb[L[sl]]=[0.45,0.42,0.38]
phys=Image.fromarray((rgb*255).astype(np.uint8)).resize((450,400))
dd=ImageDraw.Draw(phys); dd.text((6,6),'swell physics: blue = wave height, red = breaking,\nwhite = crest lines (every period)',fill=(255,255,255))
crops=[('stack: surge collar on the weather face, jade bubble\npool, filaments curling into the lee',0.3,(720,750)),
       ('reef heads < 2 m: crescent breaks, each with a halo;\nthe foam outline mirrors the reef',0.3,(840,640)),
       ('headland: bigger waves converge on the point;\nwhite rim, streaks drawn off by the current',1.0,(560,520)),
       ('cove: waves spread and shrink; a quiet bay,\nsurf only on the reef heads and the steep faces',1.0,(900,760)),
       ('old foam streaks drifting on the current',1.0,(700,900)),
       ('the whole cove at region scale',3.0,(720,750))]
W,Hh=225,200
cells=[phys]
for lab,m,(cx,cy) in crops:
    cam=Cam(cx=cx,cy=cy,mpp=m,W=W,H=Hh,yaw=90,elev=30,zc=0.45)
    img,G=render(S,zl,cam,params=params('sun','swell'))
    im=Image.fromarray(img).resize((W*2,Hh*2),Image.NEAREST)
    di=ImageDraw.Draw(im); di.rectangle([0,0,W*2,30],fill=(18,18,22)); di.text((4,2),lab+f'  [{m} m/px]',fill=(235,235,235))
    cells.append(im)
out=Image.new('RGB',(3*460,3*410+30),(18,18,22)); do=ImageDraw.Draw(out)

Bash: Append method section to LESSONS
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/elements/sea; cat >> LESSONS.md <<'EOF'

---

## 2. Translation: the method

### 2.1 The one idea

**The sea draws itself from a simulation a game already has, and the painter only chooses marks.** The
brief's warning about the conifers study (close trees and stands as two techniques) is avoided by making
every scale read the *same fields*: swell height and phase, three foam quantities, current, kelp, depth
and bottom. The iso 0.3 m/px panel and the 3 m/px panel differ only in which marks a field is allowed to
make at that pixel size.



depth, z (1 m lidar)  ──► waves.swell (rays: H, breaking)  +  waves.eikonal (crest travel time t)
                      ──► sim.SeaState: current (potential flow + eddies), kelp, surge collar,
                          60 wave periods of breaking -&gt; W, A (grids), foam particles (F)
                      ──► paint.paint_sea(G-buffer, fields, camera) -&gt; (ramp, step) index canvas
                      ──► pal.build(light) -&gt; RGB     (palette is the light: 5 states, one canvas)




Code: `src/sea/` (waves.py, sim.py, paint.py, pal.py, render.py, scene.py, place.py, noise.py).
Drivers: `stage1.py` (photo | mine at one scale), `lockstep.py` (0.1/0.3/1/3 m/px beside the photo),
`shore.py` (eye level), `sheets.py` (A, B), `sheetC.py`, `modal_sim.py` (runs sims on Modal).

### 2.2 The rules (each earned by a failure)

1. **Surf is where the reef is, not where the shore is.** Breaking is per crest, against the depth under
   it (pinnacle depth: shallowest within 5 m). The first coast lane draws rings by distance to shore; the
   photo draws the bathymetry (Gilland's "outline of foam mirrors the reef").
2. **Crests are travel-time contours**, so a breaking crest leaves a *crescent*: sharp on its shoreward
   leading edge, fading seaward (W decays for τ≈6–8 s as the crest moves on). Nothing places a crescent.
3. **Foam is coverage, not stipple.** First versions dithered foam density per pixel (salt, "confetti" in
   the water study's critique). Now foam = coverage > a *smooth* threshold field whose features are ≥ 3–4
   px, so edges are ragged but solid. Holes (Ruskin's oval gaps) appear only when they are ≥ 6 px across.
4. **Every texture scale is quantised in octaves against pixel size** (`cq(base_m, mpp, k)`): a feature is
   base metres or k pixels, whichever is larger, rounded up to a power of two so zooming doesn't swim.
5. **Never rotate a noise field per pixel.** Aligning foam texture with the local current (which swirls
   round every rock and eddy) turned the storm into marbled end-paper. One global direction (the mean
   swell heading) per frame; the *fields* carry the local shapes.
6. **Old foam must live as particles.** Eulerian advection blurred streaks to fog in minutes; particles
   (two lifetimes, 25 s and 450 s) keep them thin and long, and give close views their own fine splat.
7. **Calibrate foam against the photo's white fraction** at the same view and pixel size: photo 4.6%,
   mine 4.7% at `foam_gain` 0.3 (`src/calib.py`).
8. **Depth bands come from a smoothed depth** (σ 6 m), or the 1 m lidar's roughness prints camouflage.
9. **Kelp is a mat where canopy cover is high and specks in groups where it is low**; mats are
   rank-uniformised noise so their area equals the expected cover.
10. **The aerated pool is its own ramp** (measured L\* 64–77, a\* −16): the value between sea and foam that
    makes a stack's surf read. Without it: white collar straight onto blue (critic, round 1).
11. **At eye level the water is a heightfield.** Faces occlude what's behind them; each facet's Fresnel
    uses its own normal, so faces toward the eye darken and backs mirror the horizon (Ruskin's "dark
    continuous furrows"). Ray hits are refined by bisection, or the march leaves vertical stair stripes.
12. **Surf-zone foam is phased by the bores:** densest just behind each bore, thinning to lace until the
    next (one band per wave, not uniform marbling).

### 2.3 Parameters (defaults in `paint_sea`, `SeaState`, `swell`)

| parameter | default | what it does |
|---|---|---|
| `Hs, T, dir_from` | 1.6 m, 8.3 s, 281° | offshore swell (NDBC) |
| `gamma` (BJ), `gamma_i` | 0.8, 0.62 | random-wave and per-crest breaking ratios |
| `tau_w, tau_a, tau_f, tau_short, long_frac` | 6, 14, 450, 25 s, 0.3 | whitewater, aeration, long and short foam lives |
| `shed` | 7.5 | foam particles per unit of decaying whitewater |
| `current, eddy` | (0.05, −0.25) m/s, 0.12 m/s | drift steered round land + eddies |
| `wind` | (u, v) m/s | whitecaps (Monahan), windrows (> 5 m/s), glitter slope |
| `kelp_amount, mat_rho` | place-dependent, 0.22 | kelp canopy; mat threshold |
| `foam_gain` | 0.3 | particle density → coverage (calibrated) |
| `lace_cell, lump_cell` | 2.2, 0.9 m | foam hole and lump sizes before LOD |
| light | sun / overcast / golden / dusk / fog | palette only |

**Resources.** Each sim is ~2 CPU × 6–8 min and ~1.5 GB RAM on Modal (`voxsim-study-sea-sim`); the 9-job
sweep plus ~8 test runs used about 3 CPU-hours (roughly $0.5). Locally, a render is 5–20 s at one core.
Nothing needs much memory; the particle lists (≤ 4 M floats) are the largest thing. Cache kept under
~0.6 GB (`cache/`, float16 fields).
EOF
echo ok

# output:
ok

### 2.4 How it got here (stages, failures first)

All in `stages/`; each `s1_poa_iso_vN.png` is photo (warped to the same 30° view, box-averaged) | mine.

- **v1** (`s1_poa_iso.png`): the body and kelp only. Kelp as dark camouflage over half the cove (the
  bed noise had no gaps), foam invisible. Colour already within ~2 L\* of the photo's water median.
- **v2–v3**: surge collar added; kelp coverage made to equal expected canopy (rank-uniformised). Too
  little white, and the reef-head crescents missing: the ray-traced cove had *no* breaking, because the
  ray-tube width counted rays that had died on rocks (a fake 5× spreading). Fixed.
- **v4–v5**: pinnacle depth for breaking; crescents appear on reef heads; foam still smeared into fog by
  Eulerian advection → foam particles.
- **v6–v8** (sims moved to Modal; the laptop's load average was 100+ with ~30 lanes): eddies and
  long-lived foam; white fraction calibrated (photo 4.6% | mine 4.7%).
- **Lockstep** (`s1_poa_lockstep_v1..v7`, `s2_poa_lockstep.png`, 0.1/0.3/1/3 m/px): critic round 1 named
  pill-shaped foam blobs, missing jade pool, flat vector halos, camouflage open water, kelp as noise.
  Fixed: smoothed-depth bands, measured aerated-pool ramp, crest-aligned foam noise, grouped kelp specks.
- **Eye level** (`s1_poa_shore*.png`): v1 upside down (a left-handed grid; up = −right×forward);
  v2–v4 a snowfield of foam with no waves; v5 the water became a heightfield (faces, backs, occlusion);
  v6 bisection removed the stair-step columns.
- **Sweep sheets** (`sheets/A_*`, `B_*`, `C_*`): critic round 2 found the storm row "marbled end-paper",
  rows too alike, kelp too navy. Cause of the marbling: every Worley/fbm texture was rotated per pixel by
  the local current, which swirls round rocks and eddies. One global direction per frame fixed it
  (`notes/dbg_storm_kala.png` before/after in the notebook trail).

### 2.5 Sheets (deliverables)

- `sheets/A_state_x_bottom_iso1.png`, `_iso3.png`, `_shore.png`: sea state (calm Hs 0.6 m/7 s; swell
  1.6 m/8.3 s; storm 5.5 m/14 s + 18 m/s wind) × bottom (Kalaloch sand, Point of the Arches rock reef,
  Cape Alava kelp), real lidar at each place, sun.
- `sheets/B_light_x_view_poa.png`, `_alava.png`: sun, overcast, golden, dusk, fog × iso 1 m/px, iso 3
  m/px, eye level. One index canvas per column; only the palette changes between rows.
- `sheets/C_surf_vocabulary.png`: the swell physics map (height, breaking, crest lines) and labelled crops
  around the stack and headlands.
- `stages/s2_poa_lockstep.png`: the lockstep answer key, photo | mine at 0.1, 0.3, 1, 3 m/px.

## 3. Where it stands (plainly)

Against the photos, at the same view and pixel size:

- **Closest:** sea colour by depth and bottom in sun (water median L\* 47 | photo 48, a\* −8 | −11); the
  surf's *placement* (reef heads, weather faces of stacks, headlands big and coves quiet), which comes out
  of real bathymetry with nothing placed by hand; white fraction; the relighting (overcast and fog rows
  believable, critic round 2).
- **Reads, not matched:** the stack's bubble pool (right colour and size, but flat inside: the photo's
  pool is laced and churning); kelp (right places and cover, but mats are too solid and the colour is a
  compromise between my measurement, which came out navy because of surface glare, and the brown the
  critic and the ground photos expect); calm vs swell rows differ less than the photos do.
- **Weakest:**
  - **Drifting foam streaks.** The photo's 150–300 m ribbons with a crisp leading edge are the most
    characteristic thing in it; my particles make streaks 2–3× shorter and more scattered. The cause is
    probably the current: the real streaks trace a coherent jet (tidal/rip current off the reef), mine a
    weak uniform drift plus eddies. A lane with a real current field should get them for free.
  - **The storm surf zone.** No longer marbled, but it lacks the photo's broad bore bands and deep teal
    troughs: per-crest breaking breaks only ~20% of crests at Hrms ≈ 0.5 d, while the photos show every
    crest spilling across a saturated zone. `gamma_i` is the knob; the physics is honest but the look is
    not yet.
  - **Eye level.** Waves now have faces and furrows, but the near surf is busy and the beach is untextured
    (the sand is the ground study's subject; the swash sheet and glassy wet-sand mirror are not built).
- **Not done:** the San Juans (current-only sea: section 1.7 says how); animation (every mark is keyed to
  crest phase and particle age, so frames are recomputes, but I did not make clips); a Ferrari master copy
  (Ferrari's V29 surf is in `voxsim/crates/vox-paint/studies/coast/v29_surf_4x.png`; the coast lane
  already copied it).

## 4. For the lanes: what to port

1. **Run waves over your real bathymetry, not distance-to-shore.** `sea/waves.py` (rays + eikonal) is ~250
   lines of numpy; it needs only depth. Output: H, breaking fraction, crest travel time. This alone fixes
   "cartoon rings": surf moves onto the reefs and off the coves.
2. **Break per crest** (Rayleigh multiplier hashed on crest number and 30 m along-crest cells) against
   pinnacle depth, and let whitewater decay behind the crest: the crescents draw themselves.
3. **Foam as particles** shed by decaying whitewater, two lifetimes, carried by your current field.
4. **Paint coverage, not stipple;** quantise every texture size to octaves of pixel size; never rotate
   noise per pixel.
5. **Palette as light:** body measured in sun, relit as body × irradiance × tint + F × sky.

Olympic coast: POA/Cape Alava/Kalaloch runs are ready to reuse as tests (`cache/*_{calm,swell,storm}.npz`,
float16, ~30 MB each). Coast painter (`vox-paint/src/biome/coast.rs`): the Shore field's blurred distance is
the thing to replace with crest travel time and per-crest breaking.

## Resources, infrastructure

- Modal app `voxsim-study-sea-sim` (2 CPU, 8 GB, per job ~6–8 min). ~17 jobs total, ~3 CPU-hours.
- `modal run` prints `RuntimeError: aclose(): asynchronous generator is already running` after starmap
  completes (Modal client 1.2.4); results are written before it, so it is harmless.
- `imgfetch.py` failed silently the first time because the output directory's parent didn't exist.
- No memory hazards found; each local render < 1 GB.
